- **m**: Describe what this custom agent does and when to use it. ([engmostafaayman001-hub/ranch](https://github.com/engmostafaayman001-hub/ranch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/m.agent.md))
- **M&A Integration Manager**: Mergers and acquisitions integration specialist who designs and executes post-merger integration programs — covering Day 1 readiness, 100-day planning, synergy tracking, cultural integration, functional workstream coordination, and transition service agreement management. ([criptogus/agent-evolve-network](https://github.com/criptogus/agent-evolve-network/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ma-integration-manager.md) · ★235 · 18 copies)
- **M&A 整合经理**: 并购(M&A)整合专家，负责设计并执行并购后整合(PMI)项目——涵盖 Day 1 就绪、百日计划、synergy(协同效应)追踪、文化整合、职能工作流协调，以及过渡服务协议(TSA)管理。 ([lj19871202-sketch/requirement-agent-dispatch](https://github.com/lj19871202-sketch/requirement-agent-dispatch/blob/HEAD/agents/ma-integration-manager.md))
- **m-a-integration-manager**: Mergers and acquisitions integration specialist who designs and executes post-merger integration programs — covering Day 1 readiness, 100-day planning, synergy tracking, cultural integration, functional workstream coordination, and transition service agreement management. ([Bhomesh/agent-harness](https://github.com/Bhomesh/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/m-a-integration-manager.md))
- **m-a-transaktionsrecht-drafter**: Du erstellst den **Entwurf** auf Basis der Quellen, die der Researcher geliefert hat. Du argumentierst, subsumierst, formulierst Klauseln. Du triffst aber **keine** Mandatsentscheidung – die obliegt dem zugelassenen Rechtsanwalt. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/m-a-transaktionsrecht/agents/drafter.md) · ★61)
- **m-a-transaktionsrecht-researcher**: Du bist die **Recherche-Stufe** in der Researcher → Drafter → Reviewer-Pipeline. Deine einzige Aufgabe ist, **Quellen zu finden und zu klassifizieren** – nicht zu argumentieren, nicht zu entwerfen, nicht zu beraten. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/m-a-transaktionsrecht/agents/researcher.md) · ★61)
- **m-a-transaktionsrecht-reviewer**: Du bist die **Qualitäts- und Risikostufe** vor Auslieferung an den Mandatsanwalt. Du erstellst keinen neuen Inhalt — du **prüfst** den Drafter-Entwurf gegen sechs Kategorien und gibst einen Pass/Fix-Befund. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/m-a-transaktionsrecht/agents/reviewer.md) · ★61)
- **m-bug-fixer.agent**: Apply the fixes of a gap review to the migration deliverables. Use when: fix the review findings, bug fix, apply review. ([ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai](https://github.com/ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/m-bug-fixer.agent.md))
- **m-builder.agent**: Build a framed migration in Fabric format: wf-2-migration-source-dataset, wf-3-migration-data-product or wf-4-migration-dacpac. Use when: build source dataset, build data product, run stage S2, run D2, migrate dacpac. ([ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai](https://github.com/ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/m-builder.agent.md))
- **m-critic**: Plan and spec critic (opus, read-only). One pass; returns the four m-plan critic blocks and PROCEED or REJECT. ([Mola-maker/mstack](https://github.com/Mola-maker/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/m-critic.md) · ★11)
- **m-data-product.agent**: Run a whole Data Product Migration for one product, end to end: check its source datasets, frame (wf-1), build D1-D5 (wf-3) with the DACPAC (wf-4), review and fix (D6). Use when: migrate data product, migrate DW_GAIA, data product migration, dacpac. ([ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai](https://github.com/ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/m-data-product.agent.md))
- **m-data-validator.agent**: Validate the data of a migrated project, DPV1 =&gt; DPV2, live and read-only: inventory, schema, row counts, column profiles, keys, full or random-row data compare, deployment drift, view health; writes a report with gaps, causes and fixes. Use when: validate data, compare DPV1 and Fabric, row counts, data check, reconciliation, sign-off. ([ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai](https://github.com/ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/m-data-validator.agent.md))
- **m-debugger**: Root-cause debugger (sonnet). Reproduces first, ranks hypotheses, makes one fix at the origin, stops after 3 falsified hypotheses. ([Mola-maker/mstack](https://github.com/Mola-maker/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/m-debugger.md) · ★11)
- **m-explorer**: Read-only codebase scout (haiku). Finds files, symbols and relationships fast; returns paths with line numbers. Never edits. ([Mola-maker/mstack](https://github.com/Mola-maker/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/m-explorer.md) · ★11)
- **m-framer.agent**: Frame a DPV1 =&gt; Fabric migration (wf-1-framing). Use when: migrate a project, start a migration, frame GAIA, analyse a source dataset or data product, paste a migration brief. ([ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai](https://github.com/ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/m-framer.agent.md))
- **m-implementer**: Scoped implementer (sonnet). Makes the smallest change for one subtask, inside its own worktree, and proves it with the plan's verify command. ([Mola-maker/mstack](https://github.com/Mola-maker/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/m-implementer.md) · ★11)
- **m-researcher**: Single-channel researcher (sonnet). Investigates one question through one named channel and returns cited findings; writes only its report file. ([Mola-maker/mstack](https://github.com/Mola-maker/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/m-researcher.md) · ★11)
- **m-reviewer**: Independent code and PR reviewer (opus, read-only). Severity-ranked findings with file:line; FINDING/REPRO/CLEARED lines; ends with APPROVE or REQUEST CHANGES. ([Mola-maker/mstack](https://github.com/Mola-maker/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/m-reviewer.md) · ★11)
- **m-reviewer.agent**: Review a migration: one stage against its Rules Checklist, or the whole service offering (DPV1 =&gt; DPV2 coverage, artifact issues, deployment risks, lineage, bugs). Use when: review stage, gap review, what is missing, ready to deploy. ([ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai](https://github.com/ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/m-reviewer.agent.md))
- **m-scope-refresher.agent**: Refresh the migration knowledge from the DPV1 export, on demand: hub page, knowledge base (JSON per scope), scope index and estate register. Answers what a scope depends on and what can start. Use when: refresh the hub, DPV1 changed, is the knowledge current, which sources does a product need, what can start next. ([ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai](https://github.com/ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/m-scope-refresher.agent.md))
- **m-source-dataset.agent**: Run a whole Source Dataset Migration for one source, end to end: check the knowledge base, frame (wf-1), build S1-S4 (wf-2), review and fix (S5). Use when: migrate source dataset, migrate source EASYTENDER, source dataset migration. ([ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai](https://github.com/ramithbenedict/fabric-source-ingestion-ai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/m-source-dataset.agent.md))
- **m-verifier**: Evidence-based verifier (sonnet, read-only). Runs the real checks itself and returns PASS / FAIL / INCOMPLETE with an evidence table. ([Mola-maker/mstack](https://github.com/Mola-maker/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/m-verifier.md) · ★11)

- **m2-explorer**: Use for a read-only COMPREHENSION mapping of an existing Magento 2 module or feature — maps execution paths, DI wiring, plugin/observer/preference extension points, service-contract and cross-module dependencies, and produces a Mermaid call-chain flowchart. Use before modifying unfamiliar code, during bug-fix root-cause analysis (RCA) to trace a suspect execution path, or as the inventory/comprehension step of a parallel review (run this first, then pass the map to m2-reviewer for quality judgement). Returns a structured COMPREHENSION MAP — entry points, data flow, extension points, dependenc… ([nntoan/proflow](https://github.com/nntoan/proflow/blob/HEAD/packages/magento2/agents/m2-explorer.md) · ★1)

É ouro bruto. ([raquelhandb-spec/OpenSquad_9Pilla](https://github.com/raquelhandb-spec/OpenSquad_9Pilla/blob/HEAD/squads/prospecting/agents/M2-reddit.md))
- **m2-reviewer**: Use for an independent, READ-ONLY review/audit of a Magento 2 module — either a whole module or a single review dimension (architecture/API, security, frontend/admin, testing/tooling, or performance/operations). Dispatch one agent per dimension to review large or security-sensitive modules in parallel; the caller (review) owns final synthesis (dedup, severity normalization, conflict tie-breaking). Returns findings-first results — each finding with a severity (Critical/High/Medium/Low/Info), category, `file:line` evidence, and a concrete fix. Never modifies code. Examples — "review the securit… ([nntoan/proflow](https://github.com/nntoan/proflow/blob/HEAD/packages/magento2/agents/m2-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **m2map-pre-emit-reviewer**: Independent second-pass audit of a NEWLY GENERATED or MODIFIED Metin2 map before the parent agent emits it or reports success. Cites file:line or sector:file for every finding and proposes NO fixes — it surfaces issues for the parent to address. Handles: frontend builds (FDS Engineer), backend/API work (Backend Engineer), product scoping (Product Manager), project scaffolding (cc-vite-react-ds / cc-fullstack-vite skills), and general coding (pass-through). Always matches — if no specialist applies, handles the request directly. ([LifeSG/ccube-agent-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/LifeSG/ccube-agent-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/wai/agents/maestro.agent.md) · ★3)

- **MAF (primary)**: The primary entry point for the maf-autopilot toolkit. Use this when you're not sure which specialist to invoke. @maf triages your request, calls the right MCP tool directly when one suffices, or recommends a specialist agent (@maf-migration, @maf-cross-migration, @maf-auditor, @maf-best-practice-reviewer, @maf-incident-responder, @maf-rollback, @maf-onboarding) when the task is bigger. Does NOT autonomously hand off — always tells you which agent or tool is taking over and why. ([joslat/maf-doctor](https://github.com/joslat/maf-doctor/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf.agent.md) · ★14)
- **MAF 1.3.0 Migration Agent**: Use when migrating code to Microsoft Agent Framework (MAF) 1.3.0. Handles NuGet package updates, namespace changes, AsAIAgent() patterns, sessions, context providers, chat history, middleware, workflows, executors, structured output, tool approval, observability, compaction, A2A agents, and dynamic tool expansion. ([joslat/maf-claims-fraud-guardian](https://github.com/joslat/maf-claims-fraud-guardian/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-migration.agent.md) · ★13)
- **MAF Auditor Agent**: Use when you need to audit a .NET codebase and generate a ready-to-execute MAF migration plan. Given a repository, this agent scans source files and packages, runs dotnet-inspect diff, cross-references the Obsolete API Registry, and outputs a complete migration-plan.md with a populated tracking table. ([joslat/maf-doctor](https://github.com/joslat/maf-doctor/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-auditor.agent.md) · ★14)
- **MAF Best-Practice Reviewer**: Use when you need a best-practice / drift audit of a clean MAF 1.3.0 codebase. Distinct from @maf-auditor (which generates a migration plan). This reviewer assumes you are ALREADY on 1.3.0 and asks 'is this code idiomatic, secure, observable, and identity-safe?' Output: audit-report.md, NOT migration-plan.md. ([joslat/maf-doctor](https://github.com/joslat/maf-doctor/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-best-practice-reviewer.agent.md) · ★14)
- **MAF Cross-Framework Migration Agent**: Use when migrating an app FROM Semantic Kernel (C#) TO Microsoft Agent Framework (MAF) — a cross-framework port, NOT a MAF version upgrade. Inventories SK usage, classifies every construct (🌉 bridge / 🔁 rewrite / 🏗 re-architect), writes a plan, scaffolds a NEW MAF project beside the original (non-destructive), and ports it construct-by-construct with build gates. For 'upgrade MAF 1.x → 1.y' use @maf-migration instead. ([joslat/maf-doctor](https://github.com/joslat/maf-doctor/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-cross-migration.agent.md) · ★14)
- **MAF Incident Responder**: Use when a deployed MAF 1.3.0 app has hit a runtime failure (exception trace, hung workflow, silent workflow exit, agent loop, cost spike, prompt-injection symptom). Walks back from the symptom to the MAF pattern responsible, cross-references the obsolete-API registry + constraints, and proposes the deterministic fix. NOT for build-time errors — that's `@maf-auditor` / `MafRunCs0618Hunt`. ([joslat/maf-doctor](https://github.com/joslat/maf-doctor/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-incident-responder.agent.md) · ★14)
- **MAF Migration Agent**: Use when migrating a .NET codebase to Microsoft Agent Framework (MAF) 1.3.0. Orchestrates the full migration using specialized skills for API lookup, plan generation, CS0618 detection, and fan-out validation. Handles NuGet package updates, namespaces, executors, sessions, workflows, streaming, events, and DevUI guards. ([joslat/maf-doctor](https://github.com/joslat/maf-doctor/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-migration.agent.md) · ★14)
- **MAF Onboarding Guide**: Use when a developer is joining a MAF 1.3.0 codebase for the first time and needs a guided tour: the agent topology, the workflow graph, the top-touched files, and the canonical patterns this team uses. Output: a personalised onboarding brief, not a generic README. ([joslat/maf-doctor](https://github.com/joslat/maf-doctor/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-onboarding.agent.md) · ★14)
- **MAF Py-Expert**: Create, update, refactor, explain or work with code using the Python version of Microsoft Agent Framework. ([alexander-kastil/agentic-sw-engineering](https://github.com/alexander-kastil/agentic-sw-engineering/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-python.agent.md) · ★4)
- **MAF Rollback Agent**: Use when a MAF 1.3.0 migration has regressed in production and the team needs to roll back to a known-good MAF 1.2.0 baseline surgically (not via blanket `git revert`). - **maf-infra-advisor**: MAF の Cosmos DB 連携と OpenTelemetry 可観測性に関する実装支援・レビューを行う。 ([MizuRyu/maf-hands-on](https://github.com/MizuRyu/maf-hands-on/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-infra-advisor.agent.md) · ★1)
- **maf-reviewer**: MAF ベストプラクティスに基づいてコード変更をレビューする。MAF 固有の設計・実装パターンの準拠を検証する。 ([MizuRyu/maf-hands-on](https://github.com/MizuRyu/maf-hands-on/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-reviewer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **maf-workflow-advisor**: MAF のワークフロー（Sequential, Concurrent, Handoff, WorkflowBuilder）に関する実装支援・レビューを行う。 ([MizuRyu/maf-hands-on](https://github.com/MizuRyu/maf-hands-on/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/maf-workflow-advisor.agent.md) · ★1)

- **maint-agent**: 1. **Bug 修复**：接收用户反馈和 test-agent 报告，修复已交付项目的 Bug 2. **依赖更新**：定期检查 npm/pip 依赖更新，评估兼容性风险 3. **安全补丁**：响应 `npm audit` / `pip audit` 漏洞报告，及时打补丁 4. **性能优化**：根据用户反馈优化慢接口、大文件加载等问题 5. **文档同步**：修复后同步更新 README、CHANGELOG、API 文档 6. **版本发布**：修复积累到一定量后，打 tag 发新版本（如 v1.0.1 → v1.1.0） 7. **兼容性维护**：确保项目在新版 Node/Python/Docker 环境下正常运行 ([meizhiyuannnnnn/Twisty-Hope](https://github.com/meizhiyuannnnnn/Twisty-Hope/blob/HEAD/agents/maint-agent.md) · ★1)
